<template>
  <div class="call-manager-container">
    <el-card shadow="never" class="hero-card">
      <div class="hero-content">
        <div class="hero-text">
          <el-icon :size="28" color="var(--el-color-primary)"><component :is="Video" /></el-icon>
          <div>
            <h2>鏅鸿兘閫氳瘽绠＄悊</h2>
            <p>澶勭悊瀛︾敓鐨勯€氳瘽棰勭害</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="hero-actions">
          <el-button type="primary" @click="fetchCalls" :loading="loading">
            <el-icon class="el-icon--left"><component :is="RefreshCw" /></el-icon>鍒锋柊鍒楄〃
          </el-button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </el-card>

    <el-card shadow="never" class="list-card">
      <el-tabs v-model="activeTab">
        <el-tab-pane label="寰呮帴閫? name="PENDING"></el-tab-pane>
        <el-tab-pane label="宸叉帴閫? name="ACCEPTED"></el-tab-pane>
        <el-tab-pane label="宸茬粨鏉? name="FINISHED"></el-tab-pane>
      </el-tabs>

      <el-table :data="filteredCalls" style="width: 100%" v-loading="loading">
        <el-table-column prop="id" label="ID" width="80" />
        <el-table-column prop="topic" label="閫氳瘽浜嬬敱" min-width="200" />
        <el-table-column prop="targetPerson" label="鎸囧畾鑱旂郴浜? width="120" />
        <el-table-column prop="studentId" label="鍙戣捣瀛︾敓ID" width="120" />
        <el-table-column prop="createTime" label="棰勭害鏃堕棿" width="180">
          <template #default="scope">{{ scope.row.createTime ? scope.row.createTime.replace('T', ' ') : '' }}</template>
        </el-table-column>
        <el-table-column label="鎿嶄綔" width="120" fixed="right">
          <template #default="scope">
            <el-button v-if="scope.row.status === 'PENDING'" type="primary" link @click="updateStatus(scope.row, 'ACCEPTED')">鎺ラ€?/el-button>
            <el-button v-if="scope.row.status === 'ACCEPTED'" type="success" link @click="updateStatus(scope.row, 'FINISHED')">缁撴潫閫氳瘽</el-button>
            <span v-if="scope.row.status === 'FINISHED'" style="color: var(--el-color-info);">宸插畬缁?/span>
          </template>
        </el-table-column>
      </el-table>
    </el-card>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, computed, onMounted } from 'vue'
import { Video, RefreshCw } from '@lucide/vue'
import { ElMessage, ElMessageBox } from 'element-plus'
import { getCalls, saveCall } from '../../api/call'

const calls = ref([])
const activeTab = ref('PENDING')
const loading = ref(false)

const filteredCalls = computed(() => {
  return calls.value.filter(c => c.status === activeTab.value)
})

const fetchCalls = async () => {
  loading.value = true
  try {
    const res = await getCalls()
    calls.value = res || []
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error('鑾峰彇鍒楄〃澶辫触')
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

const updateStatus = async (row, newStatus) => {
  const actionText = newStatus === 'ACCEPTED' ? '纭鎺ラ€氳閫氳瘽锛? : '纭缁撴潫璇ラ€氳瘽锛?
  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm(actionText, '鎻愮ず', { confirmButtonText: '纭畾', cancelButtonText: '鍙栨秷', type: 'warning' })
    
    await saveCall({
      id: row.id,
      status: newStatus
    })
    ElMessage.success('鎿嶄綔鎴愬姛')
    fetchCalls()
  } catch (e) {
    if (e !== 'cancel') ElMessage.error('鎿嶄綔澶辫触')
  }
}

onMounted(() => {
  fetchCalls()
})
</script>

<style scoped>
.call-manager-container { max-width: 1200px; margin: 0 auto; }
.hero-card { margin-bottom: 24px; border-radius: 12px; background: linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(var(--el-color-primary-rgb), 0.1), rgba(var(--el-color-primary-rgb), 0.02)); border: none; }
.hero-content { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; }
.hero-text { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 16px; }
.hero-text h2 { margin: 0 0 4px; font-size: 22px; color: var(--text); }
.hero-text p { margin: 0; color: var(--sub); font-size: 14px; }
.list-card { border-radius: 12px; border: 1px solid var(--border); }
</style>
<template>
  <div class="checkin-container">
    <el-row :gutter="24">
      <!-- Left Column -->
      <el-col :span="18">
        <!-- Hero Header -->
        <el-card shadow="never" class="hero-card">
          <div class="hero-content">
            <div class="hero-text">
              <el-icon :size="28" color="var(--el-color-primary)"><component :is="User" /></el-icon>
              <div>
                <h2>鍏ヤ綇绠＄悊</h2>
                <p>瑙勮寖鐧昏 路 鍔ㄦ€佹祦杞?/p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <el-button type="primary" @click="openCheckinDialog"><el-icon class="el-icon--left"><component :is="Plus" /></el-icon>鍔炵悊鏂板叆浣?/el-button>
          </div>
        </el-card>

        <!-- Checkin Dialog -->
        <el-dialog v-model="checkinVisible" title="鍔炵悊鏂板叆浣? width="500px" destroy-on-close>
          <el-form :model="form" label-width="90px">
            <el-form-item label="閫夋嫨瀛︾敓" required>
              <el-select v-model="form.studentId" placeholder="璇烽€夋嫨鏈叆浣忓鐢? filterable style="width: 100%" @change="onStudentChange">
                <el-option v-for="u in unassignedStudents" :key="u.id" :label="`${u.name} (${u.gender}) - ${u.username}`" :value="u.id" />
              </el-select>
            </el-form-item>
            <el-form-item label="鐩爣妤兼爧" required>
              <el-select v-model="form.buildingId" placeholder="閫夋嫨妤兼爧" @change="onBuildingChange" style="width: 100%" :disabled="!form.studentId">
                <el-option v-for="b in availableBuildings" :key="b.id" :label="b.name" :value="b.id" />
              </el-select>
            </el-form-item>
            <el-form-item label="鐩爣鎴块棿" required>
              <el-select v-model="form.roomId" placeholder="閫夋嫨鎴块棿" @change="onRoomChange" style="width: 100%" :disabled="!form.buildingId">
                <el-option v-for="r in currentRooms" :key="r.id" :label="r.roomNumber" :value="r.id" />
              </el-select>
            </el-form-item>
            <el-form-item label="绌洪棽搴婁綅" required>
              <el-select v-model="form.bedId" placeholder="閫夋嫨搴婁綅" style="width: 100%" :disabled="!form.roomId">
                <el-option v-for="b in currentBeds" :key="b.id" :label="`${b.bedNumber}鍙峰簥`" :value="b.id" />
              </el-select>
            </el-form-item>
          </el-form>
          <template #footer>
            <span class="dialog-footer">
              <el-button @click="checkinVisible = false">鍙栨秷</el-button>
              <el-button type="primary" @click="submitCheckin" :loading="submitting">纭鍏ヤ綇</el-button>
            </span>
          </template>
        </el-dialog>

        <!-- Resident List Section -->
        <el-card shadow="never" class="list-card">
          <div class="list-header">
            <span class="list-title">鍦ㄤ綇浜哄憳</span>
            <div class="list-actions">
              <el-input v-model="search" placeholder="濮撳悕/瀛﹀彿/鎴块棿鍙? prefix-icon="Search" style="width: 240px" />
              <el-select v-model="statusFilter" style="width: 120px; margin-left: 12px">
                <el-option label="鍦ㄤ綇" value="active" />
                <el-option label="宸查€€瀹? value="inactive" />
              </el-select>
              <el-button icon="Refresh" circle style="margin-left: 12px" />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="resident-list" v-loading="loading">
            <div v-for="r in residents" :key="r.id" class="resident-item">
              <el-avatar :size="48" :src="r.avatar">{{ r.name?.[0] }}</el-avatar>
              
              <div class="resident-info">
                <div class="resident-main">
                  <span class="resident-name">{{ r.name }}</span>
                  <span class="resident-id">{{ r.id }}</span>
                  <el-tag size="small" type="primary" effect="plain" round>{{ r.status }}</el-tag>
                </div>
                <div class="resident-meta">
                  <span class="meta-item"><el-icon><component :is="Home" /></el-icon> {{ r.room }}</span>
                  <span class="meta-item"><el-icon><component :is="CalendarDays" /></el-icon> 鍏ヤ綇: {{ r.date }}</span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div class="resident-actions">
                <el-button type="danger" link @click="handleCheckout(r)">閫€瀹垮姙鐞?/el-button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </el-card>
      </el-col>

      <!-- Right Column -->
      <el-col :span="6">
        <!-- Data Dashboard -->
        <el-card shadow="never" class="side-card">
          <template #header>
            <div class="card-header"><span>鏁版嵁鐪嬫澘</span></div>
          </template>
          <div class="dashboard-list">
            <div class="dash-item">
              <div class="dash-icon bg-light-blue"><el-icon><component :is="User" /></el-icon></div>
              <div class="dash-info">
                <div class="dash-label">绯荤粺鍐呮€诲鐢?/div>
                <div class="dash-value">{{ totalStudents }} <span>浜?/span></div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="dash-item">
              <div class="dash-icon bg-light-orange"><el-icon><component :is="Check" /></el-icon></div>
              <div class="dash-info">
                <div class="dash-label">褰撳墠鍦ㄤ綇</div>
                <div class="dash-value">{{ currentOccupied }} <span>浜?/span></div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="dash-item">
              <div class="dash-icon bg-gray"><el-icon><component :is="X" /></el-icon></div>
              <div class="dash-info">
                <div class="dash-label">鏈垎閰嶅簥浣?绌洪棽)</div>
                <div class="dash-value">{{ unassignedCount }} <span>浜?/span></div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </el-card>

        <!-- Building Occupation -->
        <el-card shadow="never" class="side-card">
          <template #header>
            <div class="card-header"><span>妤兼爧鍗犵敤鎯呭喌</span></div>
          </template>
          <div class="occupation-list">
            <div class="occ-item" v-for="occ in buildingOccupations" :key="occ.id">
              <div class="occ-head">
                <span>{{ occ.name }}</span>
                <span class="text-blue">{{ occ.percentage }}%</span>
              </div>
              <el-progress :percentage="occ.percentage" :show-text="false" color="#3b82f6" />
            </div>
            <div v-if="buildingOccupations.length === 0" style="color:var(--sub); font-size:13px">鏆傛棤妤兼爧鏁版嵁</div>
          </div>
        </el-card>

        <!-- Notes -->
        <el-card shadow="never" class="side-card bg-light-blue notes-card">
          <template #header>
            <div class="card-header">
              <span>鍔炵悊椤荤煡</span>
              <el-icon><component :is="Info" /></el-icon>
            </div>
          </template>
          <ol class="tips-list">
            <li>鍏ヤ綇鍓嶉渶鏍稿瀛︾敓韬唤骞剁‘璁ゅ畬鎴愭敞鍐屻€?/li>
            <li>搴婁綅鍒嗛厤閬靛惊绯荤粺瑙勫垝锛屼弗绂佺鑷皟鎹€?/li>
            <li>閫€瀹块』瀹炲湴妫€鏌ヨ鏂藉強閽ュ寵鍥炴敹鎯呭喌銆?/li>
          </ol>
        </el-card>
      </el-col>
    </el-row>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, computed, onMounted } from 'vue'
import { User, Plus, Search, RefreshCw as Refresh, Home, CalendarDays, Check, X, Info } from '@lucide/vue'
import { getBuildings } from '../../api/building'
import { getRooms } from '../../api/room'
import request from '../../utils/request'
import { ElMessage, ElMessageBox } from 'element-plus'
import { useUserStore } from '../../store/user'

const userStore = useUserStore()
const search = ref('')
const statusFilter = ref('active')
const residents = ref([])
const loading = ref(false)

const checkinVisible = ref(false)
const submitting = ref(false)
const form = ref({ studentId: null, buildingId: null, roomId: null, bedId: null })

const buildings = ref([])
const allRooms = ref([])
const allBeds = ref([])
const unassignedStudents = ref([])
const currentRooms = ref([])
const currentBeds = ref([])

const totalStudents = ref(0)
const currentOccupied = ref(0)
const unassignedCount = ref(0)

const fetchResidents = async () => {
  loading.value = true
  try {
    const [bedsRes, roomsRes, buildingsRes, usersRes] = await Promise.all([
      request({ url: '/bed/list', method: 'get' }),
      getRooms(), 
      getBuildings(),
      request({ url: '/user/list', method: 'get' })
    ])
    const beds = bedsRes || []
    const rooms = roomsRes || []
    allBeds.value = beds
    allRooms.value = rooms
    buildings.value = buildingsRes || []
    const users = usersRes || []
    const me = userStore.userInfo || {}
    const managerGender = (me.role === 'dormmanager' && me.gender) ? me.gender : null
    
    const roomMap = Object.fromEntries(rooms.map(r => [r.id, r]))
    const buildingMap = Object.fromEntries(buildings.value.map(b => [b.id, b.name]))
    
    const occupiedBeds = beds.filter(b => b.studentId && b.status === 'OCCUPIED')
    const assignedStudentIds = new Set(occupiedBeds.map(b => b.studentId))
    
    // Find students who are not assigned, filtered by manager gender
    const studentUsers = users.filter(u => u.role === 'student' && (!managerGender || u.gender === managerGender))
    const validStudentIds = new Set(studentUsers.map(u => u.id))
    unassignedStudents.value = studentUsers.filter(u => !assignedStudentIds.has(u.id))
    
    const myOccupiedBeds = occupiedBeds.filter(b => validStudentIds.has(b.studentId))
    
    totalStudents.value = studentUsers.length
    currentOccupied.value = myOccupiedBeds.length
    unassignedCount.value = unassignedStudents.value.length
    
    residents.value = myOccupiedBeds.map(bed => {
      const room = roomMap[bed.roomId] || {}
      const bName = buildingMap[room.buildingId] || ''
      return {
        id: bed.studentId || '鏈煡',
        name: bed.studentName || '鏈煡',
        room: `${bName} 路 ${room.roomNumber || '?'} 路 ${bed.bedNumber}`,
        date: bed.createTime ? bed.createTime.substring(5, 10) : '09/01',
        status: '鍦ㄤ綇',
        avatar: '',
        bedId: bed.id
      }
    })
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error('鑾峰彇浣忓淇℃伅澶辫触')
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

const buildingOccupations = computed(() => {
  if (!buildings.value.length || !allRooms.value.length || !allBeds.value.length) return []
  return buildings.value.map(b => {
    const bRooms = allRooms.value.filter(r => r.buildingId === b.id).map(r => r.id)
    const bBeds = allBeds.value.filter(bed => bRooms.includes(bed.roomId))
    const total = bBeds.length
    const occupied = bBeds.filter(bed => bed.studentId && bed.status === 'OCCUPIED').length
    const percentage = total === 0 ? 0 : Math.round((occupied / total) * 100)
    return {
      id: b.id,
      name: b.name,
      percentage
    }
  })
})

onMounted(() => fetchResidents())

const openCheckinDialog = () => {
  form.value = { studentId: null, buildingId: null, roomId: null, bedId: null }
  currentRooms.value = []
  currentBeds.value = []
  checkinVisible.value = true
}

const availableBuildings = computed(() => {
  if (!form.value.studentId) return []
  const student = unassignedStudents.value.find(s => s.id === form.value.studentId)
  if (student && student.gender) {
    if (student.gender === '鐢?) {
      return buildings.value.filter(b => (b.type || '').includes('鐢?))
    } else if (student.gender === '濂?) {
      return buildings.value.filter(b => (b.type || '').includes('濂?))
    }
  }
  return buildings.value
})

const onStudentChange = () => {
  form.value.buildingId = null
  form.value.roomId = null
  form.value.bedId = null
  currentRooms.value = []
  currentBeds.value = []
}

const onBuildingChange = async (val) => {
  form.value.roomId = null
  form.value.bedId = null
  currentBeds.value = []
  if (!val) {
    currentRooms.value = []
    return
  }
  try {
    const rooms = await getRooms(val)
    currentRooms.value = rooms || []
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error('鑾峰彇鎴块棿澶辫触')
  }
}

const onRoomChange = async (val) => {
  form.value.bedId = null
  if (!val) {
    currentBeds.value = []
    return
  }
  try {
    const beds = await request({ url: `/bed/list?roomId=${val}`, method: 'get' })
    currentBeds.value = (beds || []).filter(b => b.status === 'EMPTY' || !b.studentId)
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error('鑾峰彇搴婁綅澶辫触')
  }
}

const submitCheckin = async () => {
  if (!form.value.studentId || !form.value.bedId) {
    ElMessage.warning('璇峰畬鏁撮€夋嫨瀛︾敓鍜屽簥浣?)
    return
  }
  submitting.value = true
  try {
    await request({ 
      url: '/bed/save', 
      method: 'post', 
      data: { id: form.value.bedId, studentId: form.value.studentId, status: 'OCCUPIED' } 
    })
    ElMessage.success('鍔炵悊鍏ヤ綇鎴愬姛')
    checkinVisible.value = false
    fetchResidents()
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error('鍏ヤ綇鍔炵悊澶辫触')
  } finally {
    submitting.value = false
  }
}

const handleCheckout = async (r) => {
  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm(`纭畾瑕佷负 ${r.name} 鍔炵悊閫€瀹垮悧锛焋, '閫€瀹跨‘璁?, { type: 'warning' })
    await request({ url: '/bed/save', method: 'post', data: { id: r.bedId, status: 'EMPTY', studentId: null } })
    ElMessage.success('閫€瀹垮姙鐞嗘垚鍔?)
    fetchResidents()
  } catch (e) {
    if (e !== 'cancel') ElMessage.error('閫€瀹挎搷浣滃け璐?)
  }
}
</script>

<template>
  <div class="workbench-page">
    <el-card shadow="never" class="page-card">
      <div class="page-heading">
        <div>
          <h2>姘寸數璁¤垂涓績</h2>
          <p>绠＄悊瀹胯垗姘寸數璐﹀崟锛屽彂甯冩柊璐﹀崟涓庣櫥璁扮即璐圭姸鎬併€?/p>
        </div>
        <el-button type="primary" @click="openPublishDialog">鍙戝竷鏂拌处鍗?/el-button>
      </div>
    </el-card>

    <el-card shadow="never" class="table-card">
      <div class="toolbar">
        <el-select v-model="filterBuilding" placeholder="绛涢€夋ゼ鏍? style="width: 150px" clearable @change="fetchBills">
          <el-option v-for="b in buildings" :key="b.id" :label="b.name" :value="b.id" />
        </el-select>
        <el-select v-model="filterStatus" placeholder="鐘舵€佺瓫閫? style="width: 120px" clearable @change="fetchBills">
          <el-option label="寰呯即璐? value="UNPAID" />
          <el-option label="宸茬粨娓? value="PAID" />
        </el-select>
        <el-button :loading="loading" @click="fetchBills">
          鍒锋柊鏁版嵁
        </el-button>
      </div>

      <el-table v-loading="loading" :data="filteredBills" style="width: 100%" empty-text="鏆傛棤璐﹀崟鏁版嵁">
        <el-table-column prop="roomName" label="瀹胯垗鍙? min-width="120" />
        <el-table-column prop="month" label="璐︽湡" min-width="100" />
        <el-table-column prop="type" label="绫诲瀷" min-width="100">
          <template #default="scope">
            <el-tag :type="scope.row.type === 'WATER' ? 'primary' : 'warning'" effect="light">
              {{ scope.row.type === 'WATER' ? '姘磋垂' : '鐢佃垂' }}
            </el-tag>
          </template>
        </el-table-column>
        <el-table-column label="閲戦" min-width="100">
          <template #default="scope">
            <strong>楼 {{ Number(scope.row.amount).toFixed(2) }}</strong>
          </template>
        </el-table-column>
        <el-table-column label="鐘舵€? min-width="100">
          <template #default="scope">
            <el-tag :type="scope.row.status === 'UNPAID' ? 'danger' : 'success'" effect="plain">
              {{ scope.row.status === 'UNPAID' ? '寰呯即璐? : '宸茬即璐? }}
            </el-tag>
          </template>
        </el-table-column>
        <el-table-column label="鎿嶄綔" width="150" align="right">
          <template #default="scope">
            <el-button 
              v-if="scope.row.status === 'UNPAID'" 
              type="success" link 
              @click="markAsPaid(scope.row)"
            >
              鐧昏宸茬即
            </el-button>
            <el-button type="danger" link @click="handleDelete(scope.row)">
              鍒犻櫎
            </el-button>
          </template>
        </el-table-column>
      </el-table>
    </el-card>

    <!-- 鍙戝竷璐﹀崟寮圭獥 -->
    <el-dialog v-model="dialogVisible" title="鍙戝竷姘寸數璐﹀崟" width="450px" destroy-on-close>
      <el-form :model="form" label-width="80px">
        <el-form-item label="鐩爣妤兼爧" required>
          <el-select v-model="form.buildingId" placeholder="閫夋嫨妤兼爧" @change="onBuildingChange" style="width: 100%">
            <el-option v-for="b in buildings" :key="b.id" :label="b.name" :value="b.id" />
          </el-select>
        </el-form-item>
        <el-form-item label="鐩爣鎴块棿" required>
          <el-select v-model="form.roomId" placeholder="閫夋嫨鎴块棿" style="width: 100%" :disabled="!form.buildingId">
            <el-option v-for="r in currentRooms" :key="r.id" :label="r.roomNumber" :value="r.id" />
          </el-select>
        </el-form-item>
        <el-form-item label="璐圭敤绫诲瀷" required>
          <el-radio-group v-model="form.type">
            <el-radio label="WATER">姘磋垂</el-radio>
            <el-radio label="ELECTRICITY">鐢佃垂</el-radio>
          </el-radio-group>
        </el-form-item>
        <el-form-item label="璐︽湡" required>
          <el-date-picker
            v-model="form.month"
            type="month"
            placeholder="閫夋嫨鏈堜唤"
            format="YYYY-MM"
            value-format="YYYY-MM"
            style="width: 100%"
          />
        </el-form-item>
        <el-form-item label="閲戦(鍏?" required>
          <el-input-number v-model="form.amount" :min="0.01" :precision="2" :step="10" style="width: 100%" />
        </el-form-item>
      </el-form>
      <template #footer>
        <span class="dialog-footer">
          <el-button @click="dialogVisible = false">鍙栨秷</el-button>
          <el-button type="primary" @click="submitBill" :loading="submitting">鍙戝竷</el-button>
        </span>
      </template>
    </el-dialog>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, onMounted, computed } from 'vue'
import { ElMessage, ElMessageBox } from 'element-plus'
import { getFeeBills, saveFeeBill, deleteFeeBill } from '../../api/fee'
import { getBuildings } from '../../api/building'
import { getRooms } from '../../api/room'

const loading = ref(false)
const bills = ref([])
const buildings = ref([])
const allRooms = ref([]) // To map building id to rooms

const filterBuilding = ref(null)
const filterStatus = ref('')

const dialogVisible = ref(false)
const submitting = ref(false)
const form = ref({
  buildingId: null,
  roomId: null,
  type: 'WATER',
  month: '',
  amount: 0.00
})
const currentRooms = ref([])

const filteredBills = computed(() => {
  let res = bills.value
  if (filterBuilding.value) {
    // We need to filter by building. roomName typically looks like "鑷冲杽妤?101"
    const bName = buildings.value.find(b => b.id === filterBuilding.value)?.name || ''
    res = res.filter(b => b.roomName && b.roomName.startsWith(bName))
  }
  return res
})

async function fetchBills() {
  loading.value = true
  try {
    const res = await getFeeBills(null, filterStatus.value || null)
    bills.value = res || []
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error('鑾峰彇璐﹀崟澶辫触')
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

async function fetchBaseData() {
  try {
    const bs = await getBuildings()
    buildings.value = bs || []
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to load buildings')
  }
}

function openPublishDialog() {
  form.value = { buildingId: null, roomId: null, type: 'WATER', month: '', amount: 0 }
  currentRooms.value = []
  dialogVisible.value = true
}

async function onBuildingChange(val) {
  form.value.roomId = null
  if (!val) {
    currentRooms.value = []
    return
  }
  try {
    const rooms = await getRooms(val)
    currentRooms.value = rooms || []
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error('鑾峰彇鎴块棿澶辫触')
  }
}

async function submitBill() {
  if (!form.value.roomId || !form.value.month || form.value.amount <= 0) {
    ElMessage.warning('璇峰畬鏁村～鍐欒处鍗曚俊鎭?)
    return
  }
  submitting.value = true
  try {
    const payload = {
      roomId: form.value.roomId,
      type: form.value.type,
      month: form.value.month,
      amount: form.value.amount,
      status: 'UNPAID'
    }
    await saveFeeBill(payload)
    ElMessage.success('璐﹀崟鍙戝竷鎴愬姛')
    dialogVisible.value = false
    fetchBills()
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error('鍙戝竷澶辫触')
  } finally {
    submitting.value = false
  }
}

async function markAsPaid(row) {
  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm(`纭璇ヨ处鍗?楼${row.amount})宸插畬鎴愮嚎涓嬬即璐癸紵`, '鎻愮ず', { type: 'warning' })
    await saveFeeBill({ ...row, status: 'PAID' })
    ElMessage.success('鐧昏鎴愬姛')
    fetchBills()
  } catch (e) {
    // cancelled
  }
}

async function handleDelete(row) {
  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm('纭畾鍒犻櫎璇ヨ处鍗曡褰曪紵姝ゆ搷浣滀笉鍙挙閿€銆?, '璀﹀憡', { type: 'danger' })
    await deleteFeeBill(row.id)
    ElMessage.success('鍒犻櫎鎴愬姛')
    fetchBills()
  } catch (e) {
    // cancelled
  }
}

onMounted(() => {
  fetchBaseData()
  fetchBills()
})
</script>

  margin: 0 auto;
}
.page-card {
  margin-bottom: 24px;
}
.page-heading {

  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;

.page-heading h2 {
  margin: 0 0 8px;
  color: var(--text);
  font-size: 22px;
}
.page-heading p {
  margin: 0;
  color: var(--sub);
  font-size: 14px;
}
.toolbar {
  display: flex;
  gap: 16px;
  margin-bottom: 20px;
}

<template>
  <div class="feedback-manager-container">
    <el-card shadow="never" class="hero-card">
      <div class="hero-content">
        <div class="hero-text">
          <el-icon :size="28" color="var(--el-color-primary)"><component :is="MessageCircle" /></el-icon>
          <div>
            <h2>鎰忚鍙嶉澶勭悊</h2>
            <p>鏌ョ湅骞跺洖澶嶅鐢熺殑鎰忚涓庢姇璇?/p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="hero-actions">
          <el-button type="primary" @click="fetchFeedbacks" :loading="loading">
            <el-icon class="el-icon--left"><component :is="RefreshCw" /></el-icon>鍒锋柊鍒楄〃
          </el-button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </el-card>

    <el-card shadow="never" class="list-card">
      <el-tabs v-model="activeTab" @tab-change="handleTabChange">
        <el-tab-pane label="寰呭洖澶? name="PENDING"></el-tab-pane>
        <el-tab-pane label="宸插洖澶? name="REPLIED"></el-tab-pane>
      </el-tabs>

      <el-table :data="filteredFeedbacks" style="width: 100%" v-loading="loading">
        <el-table-column prop="id" label="ID" width="80" />
        <el-table-column prop="title" label="鍙嶉绫诲瀷" width="120" />
        <el-table-column prop="description" label="鍙嶉鍐呭" min-width="250" show-overflow-tooltip />
        <el-table-column prop="createTime" label="鎻愪氦鏃堕棿" width="180">
          <template #default="scope">{{ scope.row.createTime ? scope.row.createTime.replace('T', ' ') : '' }}</template>
        </el-table-column>
        <el-table-column label="鎿嶄綔" width="120" fixed="right">
          <template #default="scope">
            <el-button v-if="scope.row.status === 'PENDING'" type="primary" link @click="openReplyDialog(scope.row)">鍥炲</el-button>
            <el-button v-else type="primary" link @click="viewReply(scope.row)">鏌ョ湅</el-button>
          </template>
        </el-table-column>
      </el-table>
    </el-card>

    <!-- 鍥炲寮圭獥 -->
    <el-dialog v-model="dialogVisible" :title="isViewMode ? '鏌ョ湅鍙嶉' : '鍥炲鍙嶉'" width="500px">
      <div class="feedback-detail">
        <div class="detail-item">
          <span class="label">鍙嶉绫诲瀷锛?/span>
          <span class="value">{{ currentRecord.title || currentRecord.type }}</span>
        </div>
        <div class="detail-item">
          <span class="label">鍙嶉鍐呭锛?/span>
          <span class="value" style="white-space: pre-wrap;">{{ currentRecord.description }}</span>
        </div>
        
        <div class="reply-section" v-if="!isViewMode">
          <el-divider border-style="dashed" />
          <el-input 
            v-model="replyContent" 
            type="textarea" 
            :rows="4" 
            placeholder="璇疯緭鍏ュ洖澶嶅唴瀹癸紝鍥炲鍚庡鐢熺灏嗙珛鍗虫敹鍒? 
          />
        </div>
        <div class="reply-section view-mode" v-else>
          <el-divider border-style="dashed" />
          <div class="detail-item">
            <span class="label">鎴戠殑鍥炲锛?/span>
            <span class="value" style="white-space: pre-wrap;">{{ currentRecord.reply }}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <template #footer>
        <span class="dialog-footer">
          <el-button @click="dialogVisible = false">{{ isViewMode ? '鍏抽棴' : '鍙栨秷' }}</el-button>
          <el-button v-if="!isViewMode" type="primary" @click="submitReply" :loading="submitting">鍙戦€佸洖澶?/el-button>
        </span>
      </template>
    </el-dialog>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, computed, onMounted } from 'vue'
import { MessageCircle, RefreshCw } from '@lucide/vue'
import { ElMessage } from 'element-plus'
import request from '../../utils/request'

const feedbacks = ref([])
const loading = ref(false)
const dialogVisible = ref(false)
const isViewMode = ref(false)
const currentRecord = ref({})
const replyContent = ref('')
const activeTab = ref('PENDING')
const submitting = ref(false)

const filteredFeedbacks = computed(() => {
  return feedbacks.value.filter(f => f.status === activeTab.value)
})

const fetchFeedbacks = async () => {
  loading.value = true
  try {
    const res = await request({ url: '/businessRecord/list', method: 'get', params: { type: 'feedback' } })
    feedbacks.value = res || []
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error('鑾峰彇鍒楄〃澶辫触')
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

const handleTabChange = () => {
  // tab changes handled by computed property
}

const openReplyDialog = (row) => {
  currentRecord.value = { ...row }
  replyContent.value = ''
  isViewMode.value = false
  dialogVisible.value = true
}

const viewReply = (row) => {
  currentRecord.value = { ...row }
  isViewMode.value = true
  dialogVisible.value = true
}

const submitReply = async () => {
  if (!replyContent.value.trim()) {
    ElMessage.warning('璇疯緭鍏ュ洖澶嶅唴瀹?)
    return
  }
  submitting.value = true
  try {
    await request({
      url: '/businessRecord/save',
      method: 'post',
      data: {
        ...currentRecord.value,
        reply: replyContent.value,
        status: 'REPLIED'
      }
    })
    ElMessage.success('鍥炲鎴愬姛')
    dialogVisible.value = false
    fetchFeedbacks()
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error('鍥炲澶辫触')
  } finally {
    submitting.value = false
  }
}

onMounted(() => {
  fetchFeedbacks()
})
</script>

.hero-content { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; }
.hero-text { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 16px; }

                <el-option label="鍏ㄩ儴妤兼爧" value="all" />
                <el-option label="鏄庡痉妤? value="mingde" />
                <el-option label="鑷冲杽妤? value="zhishan" />
              </el-select>
            </div>
            <div class="list-actions">
              <el-button icon="Refresh" circle style="margin-left: 12px" @click="fetchRecords" />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="record-list" v-loading="loading">
            <el-empty v-if="records.length === 0" description="鏆傛棤妫€鏌ヨ褰? />
            <div v-for="record in records" :key="record.id" class="record-item">
              <div class="score-badge" :class="getScoreClass(record.score)">
                <span class="score-val">{{ record.score }}</span>
                <span class="score-unit">鍒?/span>
              </div>
              
              <div class="record-info">
                <div class="record-main">
                  <span class="record-room">{{ record.room }}</span>
                  <el-tag size="small" :type="record.score >= 90 ? 'primary' : 'warning'" effect="plain" round>
                    {{ record.score >= 90 ? '浼樼' : (record.score >= 80 ? '鑹ソ' : '鍙婃牸') }}
                  </el-tag>
                </div>
                <div class="record-desc">{{ record.comment || record.description || '鏃犲娉? }}</div>
                <div class="record-meta">
                  <span class="meta-item"><el-icon><component :is="User" /></el-icon> {{ record.inspector }}</span>
                  <span class="meta-item"><el-icon><component :is="Clock" /></el-icon> {{ record.time }}</span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div class="record-actions">
                <el-button type="danger" link @click="handleDelete(record.id)"><el-icon class="el-icon--left"><component :is="Trash2" /></el-icon>鍒犻櫎</el-button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </el-card>
      </el-col>
    </el-row>

    <el-dialog v-model="dialogVisible" title="褰曞叆妫€鏌ョ粨鏋? width="520px">
      <el-form :model="form" label-width="88px">
        <el-form-item label="妫€鏌ユ埧闂? required>
          <el-select v-model="form.roomId" filterable placeholder="璇烽€夋嫨鎴块棿" style="width: 100%">
            <el-option
              v-for="room in rooms"
              :key="room.id"
              :label="room.buildingName ? `${room.buildingName} ${room.roomNumber}` : room.roomNumber"
              :value="room.id"
            />
          </el-select>
        </el-form-item>
        <el-form-item label="妫€鏌ュ垎鏁? required>
          <el-input-number v-model="form.score" :min="0" :max="100" style="width: 100%" />
        </el-form-item>
        <el-form-item label="妫€鏌ユ棩鏈? required>
          <el-date-picker v-model="form.checkDate" type="date" value-format="YYYY-MM-DD" placeholder="璇烽€夋嫨鏃ユ湡" style="width: 100%" />
        </el-form-item>
        <el-form-item label="妫€鏌ュ娉?>
          <el-input v-model="form.comment" type="textarea" :rows="4" placeholder="璇疯緭鍏ュ崼鐢熸儏鍐点€佹暣鏀瑰缓璁垨浜偣" />
        </el-form-item>
      </el-form>
      <template #footer>
        <el-button @click="dialogVisible = false">鍙栨秷</el-button>
        <el-button type="primary" :loading="submitting" @click="handleSubmit">淇濆瓨</el-button>
      </template>
    </el-dialog>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, onMounted, computed } from 'vue'
import { Medal, Plus, BarChart2, ArrowUp, ArrowDown, RefreshCw as Refresh, User, Clock, Trash2 } from '@lucide/vue'
import { getHygieneRecords, saveHygieneRecord, deleteHygieneRecord } from '../../api/hygiene'
import { getRooms } from '../../api/room'
import { ElMessage, ElMessageBox } from 'element-plus'
import { useUserStore } from '../../store/user'

const activeCriteria = ref('1')
const buildingFilter = ref('')
const userStore = useUserStore()

const records = ref([])
const rooms = ref([])
const loading = ref(false)
const submitting = ref(false)
const dialogVisible = ref(false)
const form = ref({
  roomId: null,
  score: 90,
  checkDate: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
  comment: ''
})

const fetchRecords = async () => {
  loading.value = true
  try {
    const res = await getHygieneRecords()
    records.value = (Array.isArray(res) ? res : (res.data || [])).map(r => ({
      ...r,
      room: r.roomName || '鏈煡鎴块棿',
      inspector: r.inspectorName || '鏈煡',
      time: r.checkDate ? r.checkDate.replace('T', ' ') : ''
    }))
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error('鑾峰彇鍗敓妫€鏌ヨ褰曞け璐?)
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

const fetchRooms = async () => {
  try {
    rooms.value = await getRooms()
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error('鑾峰彇鎴块棿鍒楄〃澶辫触')
  }
}

onMounted(() => {
  fetchRecords()
  fetchRooms()
})

const openCreateDialog = () => {
  form.value = {
    roomId: null,
    score: 90,
    checkDate: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
    comment: ''
  }
  dialogVisible.value = true
}

const handleSubmit = async () => {
  if (!form.value.roomId || form.value.score === null || !form.value.checkDate) {
    ElMessage.warning('璇峰～鍐欐埧闂淬€佸垎鏁板拰妫€鏌ユ棩鏈?)
    return
  }
  submitting.value = true
  try {
    await saveHygieneRecord({
      roomId: form.value.roomId,
      score: form.value.score,
      checkDate: form.value.checkDate,
      comment: form.value.comment,
      inspectorId: userStore.userInfo?.id || 1
    })
    ElMessage.success('褰曞叆鎴愬姛')
    dialogVisible.value = false
    fetchRecords()
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error('褰曞叆澶辫触')
  } finally {
    submitting.value = false
  }
}

const handleDelete = async (id) => {
  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm('纭畾瑕佸垹闄よ繖鏉¤褰曞悧锛?, '鎻愮ず', { type: 'warning' })
    await deleteHygieneRecord(id)
    ElMessage.success('鍒犻櫎鎴愬姛')
    fetchRecords()
  } catch (e) {
    if (e !== 'cancel') ElMessage.error('鍒犻櫎澶辫触')
  }
}

const avgScore = computed(() => {
  if (!records.value.length) return 0
  const sum = records.value.reduce((acc, curr) => acc + curr.score, 0)
  return (sum / records.value.length).toFixed(1)
})

const getScoreClass = (score) => {
  if (score >= 90) return 'score-blue'
  if (score >= 80) return 'score-orange'
  return 'score-red'
}
</script>

<script setup>
import { ref, computed, onMounted } from 'vue'
import { PackageSearch, Plus } from '@lucide/vue'
import { ElMessage, ElMessageBox } from 'element-plus'
import { getItems, saveItem } from '../../api/item'

const items = ref([])
const activeTab = ref('PENDING')
const loading = ref(false)
const dialogVisible = ref(false)
const submitting = ref(false)
const form = ref({ title: '', owner: '', description: '' })

const filteredItems = computed(() => {
  return items.value.filter(i => i.status === activeTab.value)
})

const fetchItems = async () => {
  loading.value = true
  try {
    const res = await getItems()
    items.value = res || []
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error('鑾峰彇鍒楄〃澶辫触')
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

const openAddDialog = () => {
  form.value = { title: '', owner: '', description: '' }
  dialogVisible.value = true
}

const submitAdd = async () => {
  if (!form.value.title || !form.value.owner) {
    ElMessage.warning('璇峰～鍐欏畬鏁翠俊鎭?)
    return
  }
  submitting.value = true
  try {
    await saveItem({
      itemName: form.value.title,
      studentName: form.value.owner,
      status: 'BORROWED'
    })
    ElMessage.success('鐧昏鎴愬姛')
    dialogVisible.value = false
    fetchItems()
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error('鐧昏澶辫触')
  } finally {
    submitting.value = false
  }
}

const updateStatus = async (row, newStatus) => {
  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm('纭璇ョ墿鍝佸凡褰掕繕锛?, '鎻愮ず', { type: 'warning' })
    await saveItem({ id: row.id, status: 'RETURNED' })
    ElMessage.success('鎿嶄綔鎴愬姛')
    fetchItems()
  } catch (e) {
    if (e !== 'cancel') ElMessage.error('鎿嶄綔澶辫触')
  }
}

onMounted(() => {
  fetchItems()
})
</script>

.hero-content { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; }
.hero-text { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 16px; }
.hero-text h2 { margin: 0 0 4px; font-size: 22px; color: var(--text); }
.hero-text p { margin: 0; color: var(--sub); font-size: 14px; }

            <span v-if="scope.row.status === 'ARCHIVED'" style="color: var(--el-color-info);">宸插畬缁?/span>
          </template>

        <el-form-item label="瀛︾敓濮撳悕" required>
          <el-input v-model="form.studentName" placeholder="瀛︾敓濮撳悕" />
        </el-form-item>
        <el-form-item label="瀛﹀彿/ID">
          <el-input v-model="form.studentId" placeholder="閫夊～" />
        </el-form-item>
        <el-form-item label="瀹胯垗鍙? required>
          <el-input v-model="form.roomNumber" placeholder="渚嬪锛?01" />
        </el-form-item>
        <el-form-item label="鏅氬綊鍘熷洜" required>
          <el-input v-model="form.reason" type="textarea" :rows="3" placeholder="濉啓瀛︾敓鏅氬綊鐨勫師鍥? />
        </el-form-item>
      </el-form>
      <template #footer>
        <span class="dialog-footer">
          <el-button @click="dialogVisible = false">鍙栨秷</el-button>
          <el-button type="primary" @click="submitAdd" :loading="submitting">纭鐧昏</el-button>

<script setup>
import { ref, computed, onMounted } from 'vue'
import { Clock, Plus } from '@lucide/vue'
import { ElMessage, ElMessageBox } from 'element-plus'
import { getLateReturns, saveLateReturn } from '../../api/lateReturn'

const records = ref([])
const activeTab = ref('PENDING')
const loading = ref(false)
const dialogVisible = ref(false)
const submitting = ref(false)
const form = ref({ studentName: '', studentId: '', roomNumber: '', reason: '' })

const filteredRecords = computed(() => {
  return records.value.filter(r => r.status === activeTab.value)
})

const fetchRecords = async () => {
  loading.value = true
  try {
    const res = await getLateReturns()
    records.value = res || []
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error('鑾峰彇鍒楄〃澶辫触')
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

const openAddDialog = () => {
  form.value = { studentName: '', studentId: '', roomNumber: '', reason: '' }
  dialogVisible.value = true
}

const submitAdd = async () => {
  if (!form.value.studentName || !form.value.roomNumber) {
    ElMessage.warning('璇峰～鍐欏繀濉俊鎭?)
    return
  }
  submitting.value = true
  try {
    const now = new Date()
    // format as ISO without Z to match LocalDateTime roughly
    const returnTime = new Date(now.getTime() - now.getTimezoneOffset() * 60000).toISOString().slice(0, -1)
    
    await saveLateReturn({
      studentName: form.value.studentName,
      studentId: form.value.studentId ? parseInt(form.value.studentId) : null,
      roomNumber: form.value.roomNumber,
      reason: form.value.reason,
      status: 'PENDING',
      returnTime: returnTime
    })
    ElMessage.success('鐧昏鎴愬姛')
    dialogVisible.value = false
    fetchRecords()
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error('鐧昏澶辫触')
  } finally {
    submitting.value = false
  }
}

const updateStatus = async (row, newStatus) => {
  const actionText = newStatus === 'NOTIFIED' ? '纭閫氭姤璇ヨ褰曪紵' : '纭褰掓。璇ヨ褰曪紵'
  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm(actionText, '鎻愮ず', { type: 'warning' })
    await saveLateReturn({ id: row.id, status: newStatus })
    ElMessage.success('鎿嶄綔鎴愬姛')
    fetchRecords()
  } catch (e) {
    if (e !== 'cancel') ElMessage.error('鎿嶄綔澶辫触')
  }
}

onMounted(() => {
  fetchRecords()
})
</script>

<template>

          </div>
        </div>

          <el-button type="primary" @click="openAddDialog">
            <el-icon class="el-icon--left"><component :is="Plus" /></el-icon>鍙戝竷鏂板叕鍛?          </el-button>
          <el-button @click="fetchMessages" :loading="loading">鍒锋柊</el-button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </el-card>

    <el-card shadow="never" class="list-card">
      <el-table :data="messages" style="width: 100%" v-loading="loading">
        <el-table-column prop="title" label="鍏憡鏍囬" width="200" />
        <el-table-column prop="description" label="鍏憡鍐呭" min-width="300" show-overflow-tooltip />
        <el-table-column prop="createTime" label="鍙戝竷鏃堕棿" width="180">
          <template #default="scope">{{ scope.row.createTime ? scope.row.createTime.replace('T', ' ') : '' }}</template>
        </el-table-column>
        <el-table-column label="鎿嶄綔" width="100" fixed="right">
          <template #default="scope">
            <el-popconfirm title="纭畾鍒犻櫎璇ュ叕鍛婂悧锛? @confirm="deleteMessage(scope.row.id)">
              <template #reference>
                <el-button type="danger" link>鍒犻櫎</el-button>
              </template>
            </el-popconfirm>
          </template>
        </el-table-column>
      </el-table>
    </el-card>

    <el-dialog v-model="dialogVisible" title="鍙戝竷鏂板叕鍛? width="500px">
      <el-form :model="form" label-width="80px">
        <el-form-item label="鍏憡鏍囬" required>
          <el-input v-model="form.title" placeholder="渚嬪锛氭湰鍛ㄤ簲鍋滄按閫氱煡" />
        </el-form-item>
        <el-form-item label="璇︾粏鍐呭" required>
          <el-input v-model="form.description" type="textarea" :rows="5" placeholder="璇疯緭鍏ラ€氱煡璇︽儏鍐呭" />
        </el-form-item>
      </el-form>
      <template #footer>
        <span class="dialog-footer">
          <el-button @click="dialogVisible = false">鍙栨秷</el-button>
          <el-button type="primary" @click="submitAdd" :loading="submitting">纭鍙戝竷</el-button>
        </span>
      </template>
    </el-dialog>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, onMounted } from 'vue'
import { Mail, Plus } from '@lucide/vue'
import { ElMessage } from 'element-plus'
import request from '../../utils/request'
import { useUserStore } from '../../store/user'

const userStore = useUserStore()
const messages = ref([])
const loading = ref(false)
const dialogVisible = ref(false)
const submitting = ref(false)
const form = ref({ title: '', description: '' })

const fetchMessages = async () => {
  loading.value = true
  try {
    // API in businessRecord.js
    const res = await request({ url: '/businessRecord/list', method: 'get', params: { type: 'manager_messages' } })
    messages.value = res || []
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error('鑾峰彇鍒楄〃澶辫触')
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

const openAddDialog = () => {
  form.value = { title: '', description: '' }
  dialogVisible.value = true
}

const submitAdd = async () => {
  if (!form.value.title || !form.value.description) {
    ElMessage.warning('璇峰～鍐欏畬鏁翠俊鎭?)
    return
  }
  submitting.value = true
  try {
    await request({
      url: '/businessRecord/save',
      method: 'post',
      data: {
        type: 'manager_messages',
        title: form.value.title,
        description: form.value.description,
        status: '宸插彂甯?,
        creatorId: userStore.userInfo?.id
      }
    })
    ElMessage.success('鍙戝竷鎴愬姛')
    dialogVisible.value = false
    fetchMessages()
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error('鍙戝竷澶辫触')
  } finally {
    submitting.value = false
  }
}

const deleteMessage = async (id) => {
  try {
    await request({
      url: `/businessRecord/${id}`,
      method: 'delete'
    })
    ElMessage.success('鍒犻櫎鎴愬姛')
    fetchMessages()
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error('鍒犻櫎澶辫触')
  }
}

onMounted(() => {
  fetchMessages()
})
</script>

<script setup>
import { ref, computed, onMounted } from 'vue'
import { ShieldCheck, Plus } from '@lucide/vue'
import { ElMessage, ElMessageBox } from 'element-plus'
import { getPatrols, savePatrol } from '../../api/patrol'

const records = ref([])
const activeTab = ref('PENDING')
const loading = ref(false)
const dialogVisible = ref(false)
const submitting = ref(false)
const form = ref({ buildingName: '', area: '', issue: '' })

const filteredRecords = computed(() => {
  return records.value.filter(r => r.status === activeTab.value)
})

const fetchRecords = async () => {
  loading.value = true
  try {
    const res = await getPatrols()
    records.value = res || []
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error('鑾峰彇鍒楄〃澶辫触')
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

const openAddDialog = () => {
  form.value = { buildingName: '', area: '', issue: '' }
  dialogVisible.value = true
}

const submitAdd = async () => {
  if (!form.value.buildingName || !form.value.area || !form.value.issue) {
    ElMessage.warning('璇峰～鍐欏繀濉俊鎭?)
    return
  }
  submitting.value = true
  try {
    await savePatrol({
      buildingName: form.value.buildingName,
      area: form.value.area,
      issue: form.value.issue,
      status: 'PENDING'
    })
    ElMessage.success('鎵撳崱鎴愬姛')
    dialogVisible.value = false
    fetchRecords()
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error('涓婃姤澶辫触')
  } finally {
    submitting.value = false
  }
}

const updateStatus = async (row, newStatus) => {
  try {
    await ElMessageBox.confirm('纭璇ュ贰鏌ラ棶棰樺凡瑙ｅ喅锛?, '鎻愮ず', { type: 'warning' })
    await savePatrol({ id: row.id, status: newStatus })
    ElMessage.success('鎿嶄綔鎴愬姛')
    fetchRecords()
  } catch (e) {
    if (e !== 'cancel') ElMessage.error('鎿嶄綔澶辫触')
  }
}

onMounted(() => {
  fetchRecords()
})
</script>

<script setup>
import { ref, onMounted } from 'vue'
import { User } from '@lucide/vue'
import { useUserStore } from '../../store/user'
import { ElMessage } from 'element-plus'
import request from '../../utils/request'

const userStore = useUserStore()
const form = ref({ id: null, name: '', phone: '', email: '', role: '' })
const saving = ref(false)

const loadUserInfo = () => {
  if (userStore.userInfo) {
    form.value.id = userStore.userInfo.id
    form.value.name = userStore.userInfo.name || ''
    form.value.phone = userStore.userInfo.phone || ''
    form.value.email = userStore.userInfo.email || ''
    form.value.role = userStore.userInfo.role || ''
  }
}

const handleSave = async () => {
  saving.value = true
  try {
    const res = await request({
      url: '/user/save',
      method: 'post',
      data: form.value
    })
    ElMessage.success('淇濆瓨鎴愬姛锛佷笅娆￠噸鏂扮櫥褰曟椂鐢熸晥銆?)
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error('淇濆瓨澶辫触')
  } finally {
    saving.value = false
  }
}

onMounted(() => {
  loadUserInfo()
})
</script>

        <!-- Process Dashboard -->
        <el-card shadow="never" class="side-card">
          <template #header>
            <div class="card-header"><span>澶勭悊鐪嬫澘</span></div>
          </template>
          <div class="dashboard-list">
            <div class="dash-item">
              <div class="dash-icon bg-light-orange"><el-icon><component :is="Clock" /></el-icon></div>
              <div class="dash-info">

                <div class="dash-value">{{ pendingCount }} <span>鍗?/span></div>
              </div>

<script setup>
import { ref, computed, onMounted } from 'vue'
import { Settings, RefreshCw as Refresh, Search, User, Clock, Wrench, Check, Phone } from '@lucide/vue'
import { getRepairs, saveRepair } from '../../api/repair'
import { ElMessage } from 'element-plus'

const search = ref('')
const statusFilter = ref('all')
const tickets = ref([])
const loading = ref(false)

const statusMap = { 'PENDING': '寰呭鐞?, 'PROCESSING': '澶勭悊涓?, 'COMPLETED': '宸插畬鎴? }
const statusTypeMap = { 'PENDING': 'warning', 'PROCESSING': 'primary', 'COMPLETED': 'info' }
const iconMap = { '姘寸': '姘?, '鐢靛櫒': '鐢?, '闂ㄧ獥': '闂?, '缃戠粶': '缃? }

const fetchTickets = async () => {
  loading.value = true
  try {
    const statusParam = statusFilter.value === 'all' ? '' : statusFilter.value.toUpperCase()
    const res = await getRepairs(null, statusParam)
    tickets.value = (res || []).map(t => ({
      ...t,
      categoryIcon: iconMap[t.type] || '淇?,
      category: t.type,
      room: t.roomName || '鏈煡鎴块棿',
      reporter: t.submitterName || '鏈煡',
      statusLabel: statusMap[t.status] || t.status,
      statusType: statusTypeMap[t.status] || 'info',
      time: t.createTime ? t.createTime.replace('T', ' ').substring(0, 16) : ''
    }))
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error('鑾峰彇宸ュ崟鍒楄〃澶辫触')
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

onMounted(() => fetchTickets())

const handleAccept = async (t) => {
  try {
    await saveRepair({ id: t.id, submitterId: t.submitterId, roomId: t.roomId, type: t.type, description: t.description, status: 'PROCESSING' })
    ElMessage.success('宸叉帴鍗?)
    fetchTickets()
  } catch (e) { ElMessage.error('鎿嶄綔澶辫触') }
}

const handleComplete = async (t) => {
  try {
    await saveRepair({ id: t.id, submitterId: t.submitterId, roomId: t.roomId, type: t.type, description: t.description, status: 'COMPLETED' })
    ElMessage.success('宸叉爣璁板畬宸?)
    fetchTickets()
  } catch (e) { ElMessage.error('鎿嶄綔澶辫触') }
}

const pendingCount = computed(() => tickets.value.filter(t => t.status === 'PENDING').length)
const processingCount = computed(() => tickets.value.filter(t => t.status === 'PROCESSING').length)
const completedCount = computed(() => tickets.value.filter(t => t.status === 'COMPLETED').length)
</script>

<script setup>
import { ref, computed, onMounted } from 'vue'
import { Repeat2, RefreshCw as Refresh, ArrowRight, MessageSquare, Clock, Timer, CheckCircle2, Info } from '@lucide/vue'
import { getTransfers, saveTransfer } from '../../api/transfer'
import { ElMessage } from 'element-plus'

const statusFilter = ref('all')
const applications = ref([])
const loading = ref(false)

const statusMap = { 'PENDING': '寰呭鎵?, 'APPROVED': '宸查€氳繃', 'REJECTED': '宸叉嫆缁? }
const statusTypeMap = { 'PENDING': 'warning', 'APPROVED': 'primary', 'REJECTED': 'danger' }

const fetchTransfers = async () => {
  loading.value = true
  try {
    const statusParam = statusFilter.value === 'all' ? '' : statusFilter.value.toUpperCase()
    const res = await getTransfers(null, statusParam)
    applications.value = (res || []).map(a => ({
      ...a,
      name: a.studentName || '鏈煡',
      current: a.currentBedName || '鏈煡',
      target: a.targetRoomName || '鐢卞绠″垎閰?,
      statusLabel: statusMap[a.status] || a.status,
      statusType: statusTypeMap[a.status] || 'info',
      time: a.createTime ? a.createTime.replace('T', ' ').substring(0, 16) : '',
      avatar: ''
    }))
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error('鑾峰彇璋冨鐢宠鍒楄〃澶辫触')
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

onMounted(() => fetchTransfers())

const handleApprove = async (app) => {
  try {
    await saveTransfer({ ...app, status: 'APPROVED' })
    ElMessage.success('宸叉壒鍑?)
    fetchTransfers()
  } catch (e) { ElMessage.error('鎿嶄綔澶辫触') }
}

const handleReject = async (app) => {
  try {
    await saveTransfer({ ...app, status: 'REJECTED' })
    ElMessage.success('宸查┏鍥?)
    fetchTransfers()
  } catch (e) { ElMessage.error('鎿嶄綔澶辫触') }
}

const pendingCount = computed(() => applications.value.filter(a => a.status === 'PENDING').length)
const processedCount = computed(() => applications.value.filter(a => a.status !== 'PENDING').length)
</script>

              </div>

        <!-- Visitor List Section -->

                <el-radio-button label="pending">寰呭鎵?/el-radio-button>
                <el-radio-button label="active">鍦ㄩ涓?/el-radio-button>
              </el-radio-group>
            </div>
            <div class="list-actions">
              <el-input v-model="search" placeholder="璁垮濮撳悕/鑱旂郴鐢佃瘽" prefix-icon="Search" style="width: 240px" />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="visitor-list" v-loading="loading">

              <div class="visitor-avatar" :class="v.avatarClass">
                {{ v.name?.[0] }}

              <div class="visitor-info">
                <div class="visitor-main">
                  <span class="visitor-name">{{ v.name }}</span>
                  <el-tag size="small" :type="v.status === 'APPROVED' ? 'success' : v.status === 'LEFT' ? 'info' : 'warning'" effect="plain" round>
                    {{ v.status === 'APPROVED' ? '宸叉壒鍑? : v.status === 'LEFT' ? '宸茬寮€' : '寰呭鎵? }}
                  </el-tag>
                </div>
                <div class="visitor-desc">
                  <span class="relation-tag text-blue">{{ v.relation }}</span>
                  璁块棶锛歿{ v.target }}
                </div>
                <div class="visitor-meta">
                  <span class="meta-item"><el-icon><component :is="Clock" /></el-icon> 棰勮鏉ヨ: {{ v.time }}</span>

                <el-button v-if="v.status === 'APPROVED'" type="success" size="small" @click="handleLeave(v)">鐧昏绂诲紑</el-button>
                <el-button type="primary" link>璇︽儏</el-button>
              </div>
            </div>

          <div class="pagination-wrapper">
             <span class="total-text">鍏?{{ filteredVisitors.length }} 鏉?/span>
             <el-pagination v-model:current-page="currentPage" background layout="prev, pager, next" :total="filteredVisitors.length" :page-size="pageSize" />
          </div>

            <li>绂诲紑鏃堕』杩涜绛剧鐧昏锛岀‘淇濅汉鍛樺嚭鍏ラ棴鐜€?/li>

<script setup>
import { ref, onMounted, computed } from 'vue'
import { UserCheck, RefreshCw as Refresh, Search, Clock, User, Check, Info } from '@lucide/vue'
import { getVisitorRecords, saveVisitorRecord } from '../../api/visitor'
import { ElMessage } from 'element-plus'

const search = ref('')
const statusFilter = ref('all')
const currentPage = ref(1)
const pageSize = 3

const visitors = ref([])
const loading = ref(false)

const fetchVisitors = async () => {
  loading.value = true
  try {
    const res = await getVisitorRecords()
    visitors.value = (Array.isArray(res) ? res : (res.data || [])).map(v => ({
      ...v,
      name: v.visitorName,
      target: v.studentName || '鏈煡瀛︾敓',
      time: v.visitTime ? v.visitTime.replace('T', ' ').substring(0, 16) : '',
      avatarClass: ['av-blue', 'av-green', 'av-yellow', 'av-red'][v.id % 4]
    }))
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error('鑾峰彇璁垮璁板綍澶辫触')
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

onMounted(() => fetchVisitors())

const handleApprove = async (v) => {
  try {
    await saveVisitorRecord({ id: v.id, status: 'APPROVED' })
    ElMessage.success('宸叉壒鍑嗚繘鍏?)
    fetchVisitors()
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error('鎿嶄綔澶辫触')
  }
}

const handleLeave = async (v) => {
  try {
    await saveVisitorRecord({ id: v.id, status: 'LEFT', leaveTime: new Date().toISOString() })
    ElMessage.success('宸茬櫥璁扮寮€')
    fetchVisitors()
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error('鎿嶄綔澶辫触')
  }
}

const pendingCount = computed(() => visitors.value.filter(v => v.status === 'PENDING').length)
const activeCount = computed(() => visitors.value.filter(v => v.status === 'APPROVED' && !v.leaveTime).length)
const todayCount = computed(() => visitors.value.filter(v => new Date(v.createTime).toDateString() === new Date().toDateString()).length)
const filteredVisitors = computed(() => visitors.value.filter(v => {
  const keyword = search.value.trim().toLowerCase()
  const matchesKeyword = !keyword || v.name?.toLowerCase().includes(keyword) || v.phone?.includes(keyword)
  const matchesStatus = statusFilter.value === 'all'
    || (statusFilter.value === 'pending' && v.status === 'PENDING')
    || (statusFilter.value === 'active' && v.status === 'APPROVED' && !v.leaveTime)
  return matchesKeyword && matchesStatus
}))
const pagedVisitors = computed(() => filteredVisitors.value.slice((currentPage.value - 1) * pageSize, currentPage.value * pageSize))
const recentCheckins = computed(() => visitors.value.filter(v => v.status === 'APPROVED' || v.status === 'LEFT').slice(0, 3))
</script>

                <el-select v-model="selectedBuilding" placeholder="閫夋嫨妤兼爧鏌ョ湅" style="width: 150px" size="small" clearable>
                  <el-option v-for="b in buildings" :key="b.id" :label="b.name" :value="b.id" />
                </el-select>
                <el-radio-group v-model="dynamicsView" size="small">
                  <el-radio-button label="room">鎴块棿鐘舵€?/el-radio-button>
                  <el-radio-button label="hygiene">鍗敓璇勬瘮</el-radio-button>
                </el-radio-group>
              </div>
            </div>
          </template>
          <div class="rooms-grid">
            <div v-for="room in filteredRooms" :key="room.id" class="room-box" :class="{'is-warning': room.warning}" @click="openRoomDetail(room)">
              <div class="room-number">{{ room.buildingName }}-{{ room.number }}</div>
              <div class="room-dots">
                <span v-for="i in room.capacity" :key="i" class="dot" :class="{ filled: i <= room.occupied }"></span>
              </div>
              <div v-if="room.warning" class="room-warning-icon">!</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </el-card>

        <!-- Room Detail Dialog -->
        <el-dialog v-model="roomDialogVisible" :title="`${selectedRoom?.buildingName} - ${selectedRoom?.number} 鎴块棿搴婁綅甯冨眬`" width="700px" destroy-on-close>
          <div class="layout-wrapper" v-loading="roomDetailLoading">
            <div class="floor-plan">
              <div class="plan-border"></div>
              <div class="door-label">澶ч棬</div>
              <div class="balcony-label">闃冲彴</div>
              
              <div class="beds-grid">
                <div 
                  v-for="i in (selectedRoom?.capacity || 4)" 
                  :key="i"
                  class="bed-slot"
                  :class="{ 'is-occupied': getOccupantForBed(i), 'is-empty': !getOccupantForBed(i) }"
                >
                  <div class="bed-box">
                    <div class="bed-number">{{ selectedRoom?.number ? `${selectedRoom.number}-${i}` : `1-${i}` }}</div>
                  </div>
                  <div class="bed-owner">{{ getOccupantForBed(i)?.studentName || '绌哄簥' }}</div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </el-dialog>

        <!-- Bottom Two Lists -->
        <el-row :gutter="20">
          <el-col :span="12">
            <el-card shadow="never" class="list-card">
              <template #header>
                <div class="card-header">
                  <span class="card-title">寰呭鐞嗘姤淇?/span>
                  <el-button type="primary" link>鍏ㄩ儴</el-button>
                </div>
              </template>
              <div class="mini-list">
                <el-empty v-if="pendingRepairs.length === 0" description="鏆傛棤寰呭姙" :image-size="60"></el-empty>
                <div v-for="r in pendingRepairs" :key="r.id" class="mini-item">
                  <span>{{ r.type || '鎶ヤ慨' }}</span>
                  <span class="mini-date">{{ r.createTime?.substring(0, 10) }}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </el-card>
          </el-col>
          <el-col :span="12">
            <el-card shadow="never" class="list-card">
              <template #header>
                <div class="card-header">
                  <span class="card-title">璁垮棰勭害</span>
                  <el-button type="primary" link>鍏ㄩ儴</el-button>
                </div>
              </template>
              <el-empty description="浠婃棩鏃犻绾? :image-size="80"></el-empty>
            </el-card>
          </el-col>
        </el-row>
      </el-col>

      <!-- Right Column -->

          <div class="tools-grid">

              <el-icon :size="24" color="#3b82f6"><component :is="UserPlus" /></el-icon>
              <span>鍏ヤ綇鍔炵悊</span>

                <div class="memo-title">{{ m.title }}</div>
                <div class="memo-time">{{ m.time }}</div>

              </el-icon>
              <span>{{ n.title }}</span>
            </div>
            <el-empty v-if="notices.length === 0" description="鏆傛棤閫氱煡" :image-size="40"></el-empty>

<script setup>
import { ref, computed, onMounted } from 'vue'
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { Bell, RefreshCw as Refresh, User, Settings, UserCheck, Clock, ChevronRight, UserPlus, Medal, MessageCircle, Info, AlertCircle } from '@lucide/vue'
import { getBeds, getRooms } from '../../api/room'
import { getBuildings } from '../../api/building'
import { getRepairs } from '../../api/repair'
import { getVisitorRecords } from '../../api/visitor'
import { getBusinessRecords } from '../../api/businessRecord'
import { useUserStore } from '../../store/user'
import request from '../../utils/request'
import WeatherWidget from '../../components/WeatherWidget.vue'

const router = useRouter()
const userStore = useUserStore()
const dynamicsView = ref('room')

const residentCount = ref(0)
const pendingRepairCount = ref(0)
const todayVisitorCount = ref(0)
const lateReturnCount = ref(0)
const rooms = ref([])
const buildings = ref([])
const selectedBuilding = ref('')
const pendingRepairs = ref([])
const memos = ref([])
const notices = ref([])

const roomDialogVisible = ref(false)
const selectedRoom = ref(null)
const roomDetailBeds = ref([])
const roomDetailLoading = ref(false)

const filteredRooms = computed(() => {
  if (!selectedBuilding.value) return rooms.value
  return rooms.value.filter(r => r.buildingId === selectedBuilding.value)
})

const openRoomDetail = async (room) => {
  selectedRoom.value = room
  roomDialogVisible.value = true
  roomDetailLoading.value = true
  try {
    const beds = await request({ url: `/bed/list?roomId=${room.id}`, method: 'get' })
    roomDetailBeds.value = (beds || [])
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error('鑾峰彇鎴块棿璇︽儏澶辫触')
  } finally {
    roomDetailLoading.value = false
  }
}

const getOccupantForBed = (index) => {
  const suffix = `-${index}`
  return roomDetailBeds.value.find(b => b.bedNumber && b.bedNumber.endsWith(suffix) && b.studentId)
}

const loadData = async () => {
  try {
    const [repairList, visitors, buildingList, buildingStatsRes, memoRecords, noticeRecords] = await Promise.all([
      getRepairs(), getVisitorRecords(), getBuildings(), request({ url: '/dashboard/buildings', method: 'get' }),
      getBusinessRecords('manager_memos'), getBusinessRecords('system_notices')
    ])
    
    memos.value = (memoRecords || []).slice(0, 3).map((r, i) => ({
      id: r.id,
      title: r.title,
      time: r.status || '鍏ㄥぉ',
      urgent: i === 0
    }))
    
    notices.value = (noticeRecords || []).slice(0, 3).map((r, i) => ({
      id: r.id,
      title: r.title,
      type: i === 1 ? 'alert' : 'info'
    }))
    
    buildings.value = buildingList || []
    
    // Calculate resident count from building stats
    if (buildingStatsRes) {
      residentCount.value = buildingStatsRes.reduce((acc, curr) => acc + (curr.occupiedBeds || 0), 0)
    }
    
    const pendings = repairList.filter(r => r.status === 'PENDING')
    pendingRepairCount.value = pendings.length
    pendingRepairs.value = pendings.slice(0, 3) // preview top 3
    
    const today = new Date().toDateString()
    todayVisitorCount.value = visitors.filter(v => new Date(v.createTime).toDateString() === today).length
    
    // Fetch late returns
    try {
      const lateRes = await request({ url: '/lateReturnRecord/list', method: 'get' })
      lateReturnCount.value = lateRes ? lateRes.filter(r => r.status === 'PENDING').length : 0
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to fetch late returns', err)
    }
    
    // Initial rooms load for the first building
    if (buildings.value.length > 0) {
      selectedBuilding.value = buildings.value[0].id
      await fetchRoomsForBuilding(selectedBuilding.value)
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to load workbench data', e)
  }
}

const fetchRoomsForBuilding = async (bId) => {
  if (!bId) {
    rooms.value = []
    return
  }
  try {
    const roomList = await getRooms(bId)
    rooms.value = (roomList || []).map(r => ({
      id: r.id,
      number: r.roomNumber,
      buildingId: r.buildingId,
      buildingName: r.buildingName,
      capacity: r.capacity || 4,
      occupied: r.occupied || 0,
      warning: false
    }))
  } catch(e) {
    console.error('Failed to load rooms for building', e)
  }
}

import { watch } from 'vue'
watch(selectedBuilding, (newVal) => {
  fetchRoomsForBuilding(newVal)
})

onMounted(() => loadData())
</script>

        </button>
        <button type="button" class="guide-link" @click="ask('瀹胯垗瀹夊叏鐢ㄧ數瑙勫畾鏈夊摢浜涳紵')">
          <ShieldCheck :size="17" /><span>鐢ㄧ數瀹夊叏鎸囧崡</span><ChevronRight :size="17" />

<script setup>
import { nextTick, ref } from 'vue'
import { BookOpen, ChevronRight, CircleAlert, HelpCircle, Lightbulb, Send, ShieldCheck, Wrench } from '@lucide/vue'
import request from '../../utils/request'
import { ElMessage } from 'element-plus'

const question = ref('')
const messages = ref([])
const replying = ref(false)
const messagePanel = ref(null)

const prompts = ['瀹胯垗闂ㄧ鏃堕棿', '璋冨鐢宠娴佺▼', '濡備綍鎻愪氦鎶ヤ慨', '姘寸數璐瑰浣曟煡璇?]
const popularQuestions = ['姘寸數璐硅处鍗曟煡璇?, '閬ユ帶鍣ㄩ仐澶卞鐞?, '璋冨鐢宠璧勬牸']
const rules = [
  { keys: ['闂ㄧ', '鏅氬綊'], answer: '瀹胯垗闂ㄧ閫氬父涓烘瘡鏃?23:30銆傛櫄褰掕鍙婃椂鑱旂郴瀹跨锛屽苟鎸夎姹傚畬鎴愭櫄褰掔櫥璁般€? },
  { keys: ['璋冨'], answer: '璇峰湪鈥滆皟瀹跨敵璇封€濅腑濉啓鍘熷洜骞堕€夋嫨鐩爣鎴块棿銆傚绠″鎵归€氳繃鍚庯紝绯荤粺浼氬悓姝ユ洿鏂板簥浣嶅拰瀹胯垗淇℃伅銆? },
  { keys: ['鎶ヤ慨', '缁翠慨'], answer: '璇峰湪鈥滄姤淇敵璇封€濅腑閫夋嫨鏁呴殰绫诲瀷骞舵弿杩板叿浣撴儏鍐点€傛彁浜ゅ悗鍙湪鍚屼竴椤甸潰璺熻釜澶勭悊杩涘害銆? },
  { keys: ['姘寸數璐?, '璐圭敤', '璐﹀崟'], answer: '鍙湪鈥滆垂鐢ㄦ煡璇⑩€濅腑鏌ョ湅褰撳墠瀹胯垗璐﹀崟銆傜嚎涓婄即璐瑰皻鏈紑鏀撅紝璇锋寜椤甸潰鎸囧紩鍒版湇鍔′腑蹇冨姙鐞嗐€? }
]

const ask = (text) => {
  question.value = text
  send()
}

const send = async () => {
  const content = question.value.trim()
  if (!content || replying.value) return
  messages.value.push({ id: Date.now(), role: 'user', content })
  question.value = ''
  replying.value = true
  await nextTick()
  scrollToBottom()

  try {
    const res = await request({
      url: '/ai/chat',
      method: 'get',
      params: { question: content }
    })
    messages.value.push({ id: Date.now() + 1, role: 'assistant', content: res })
  } catch (e) {
    messages.value.push({ id: Date.now() + 1, role: 'assistant', content: '鏈嶅姟寮傚父锛岃绋嶅悗鍐嶈瘯銆? })
  } finally {
    replying.value = false
    await nextTick()
    scrollToBottom()
  }
}

const scrollToBottom = () => {
  if (messagePanel.value) messagePanel.value.scrollTop = messagePanel.value.scrollHeight
}
</script>

<script setup>
import { ref, onMounted } from 'vue'
import { Video, Phone } from '@lucide/vue'
import { useUserStore } from '../../store/user'
import { ElMessage } from 'element-plus'
import request from '../../utils/request'

const userStore = useUserStore()
const form = ref({ topic: '', targetPerson: '' })
const myCalls = ref([])
const loading = ref(false)
const submitting = ref(false)

const statusMap = { 'PENDING': '寰呮帴閫?, 'ACCEPTED': '宸叉帴閫?, 'FINISHED': '宸茬粨鏉? }
const statusTypeMap = { 'PENDING': 'warning', 'ACCEPTED': 'primary', 'FINISHED': 'info' }

const fetchMyCalls = async () => {
  loading.value = true
  try {
    const res = await request({ url: '/callRecord/list', method: 'get', params: { studentId: userStore.userInfo?.id } })
    myCalls.value = res || []
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error('鑾峰彇閫氳瘽璁板綍澶辫触')
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

const handleSubmit = async () => {
  if (!form.value.topic || !form.value.targetPerson) {
    ElMessage.warning('璇峰～鍐欏畬鏁翠俊鎭?)
    return
  }
  submitting.value = true
  try {
    await request({
      url: '/callRecord/add',
      method: 'post',
      data: {
        studentId: userStore.userInfo?.id,
        topic: form.value.topic,
        targetPerson: form.value.targetPerson
      }
    })
    ElMessage.success('棰勭害鎻愪氦鎴愬姛')
    form.value.topic = ''
    form.value.targetPerson = ''
    fetchMyCalls()
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error('棰勭害鎻愪氦澶辫触')
  } finally {
    submitting.value = false
  }
}

onMounted(() => {
  if (userStore.userInfo?.id) fetchMyCalls()
})
</script>

            <div class="row" style="min-height: 60px;" v-for="t in dash.todoList" :key="t.id">
              <span class="status-dot" :style="`width: 8px; height: 8px; border-radius: 50%; background: ${t.statusColor};`"></span>

                <div class="row-title">{{ t.title }}</div>

              鏆傛棤寰呭姙浜嬮」
            </div>

              <button class="ghost-btn active-filter" style="margin-left: 8px;">鏈湀</button>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="card-body">
            <div style="display: flex; align-items: flex-end; gap: 8px; height: 120px; padding: 16px 0;">
              <div v-for="(h, i) in (dash.powerHistory || [])" :key="i" 
                   style="flex: 1; display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; gap: 4px;">
                <span style="font-size: 11px; color: var(--sub);">{{h}}</span>
                <div :style="`width: 100%; height: ${h}px; background: linear-gradient(180deg, var(--primary), var(--primary-2)); border-radius: 4px 4px 0 0; transition: height .6s ease;`"></div>
                <span style="font-size: 11px; color: var(--sub);">{{i + 1}}鏃?/span>
              </div>

<script setup>
import { ref, onMounted } from 'vue'
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { getRooms, getBeds } from '../../api/room'
import { getUsers } from '../../api/user'
import { getBuildings } from '../../api/building'
import { getBusinessRecords } from '../../api/businessRecord'
import request from '../../utils/request'
import { useUserStore } from '../../store/user'
import { 
  Home, Wallet, Settings, Medal,
  UserRoundCheck, Repeat2, MessageCircle, Pencil, Newspaper, WalletCards
} from '@lucide/vue'
import WeatherWidget from '../../components/WeatherWidget.vue'

const router = useRouter()
const userStore = useUserStore()
const hoverBanner = ref(false)
const notices = ref([])
const currentDormLabel = ref('鍔犺浇涓?)
const roommates = ref([])
const dash = ref({})

const fetchDashboard = async () => {
  try {
    const res = await request({ url: '/dashboard/student', method: 'get' })
    if (res) {
      dash.value = res
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.error(e)
  }
}

const fetchDormSummary = async () => {
  try {
    const [bedsRes, usersRes, roomsRes, buildingsRes] = await Promise.all([
      getBeds(), getUsers(), getRooms(), getBuildings()
    ])
    const beds = bedsRes || []
    const users = usersRes || []
    const rooms = roomsRes || []
    const buildings = buildingsRes || []
    const me = userStore.userInfo || {}
    const myBed = beds.find((bed) => bed.studentId === me.id)
    if (!myBed) {
      currentDormLabel.value = '鏈垎閰嶅鑸?
      roommates.value = []
      return
    }

    const room = rooms.find((item) => item.id === myBed.roomId)
    const building = room ? buildings.find((item) => item.id === room.buildingId) : null
    currentDormLabel.value = room ? `${building?.name || ''} ${room.roomNumber}`.trim() : '鏈垎閰嶅鑸?

    const userMap = Object.fromEntries(users.map((user) => [user.id, user]))
    roommates.value = beds
      .filter((bed) => bed.roomId === myBed.roomId && bed.studentId && bed.studentId !== me.id)
      .map((bed) => ({
        name: userMap[bed.studentId]?.name || '鏈煡',
        bedNumber: bed.bedNumber
      }))
  } catch (error) {
    currentDormLabel.value = '鑾峰彇澶辫触'
    roommates.value = []
  }
}

const fetchNotices = async () => {
  const records = await getBusinessRecords('manager_messages', '宸插彂甯?)
  notices.value = (records || []).slice(0, 3).map((record, index) => ({
    id: record.id,
    title: record.title,
    date: (record.createTime || '').replace('T', ' ').slice(0, 10),
    pinned: index === 0
  }))
}

onMounted(() => {
  fetchDormSummary()
  fetchNotices()
  fetchDashboard()
})
</script>

.text-truncate {
  white-space: nowrap;
  overflow: hidden;
  text-overflow: ellipsis;
}
</style>

<script setup>
import { ref, computed, onMounted } from 'vue'
import { 
  Home, MessageCircle, RefreshCw, Lightbulb, Fan, Wifi, Droplet
} from '@lucide/vue'
import { getRooms, getBeds } from '../../api/room'
import { getUsers } from '../../api/user'
import { getBuildings } from '../../api/building'
import { useUserStore } from '../../store/user'
import ChatDrawer from '../../components/ChatDrawer.vue'
import request from '../../utils/request'

const userStore = useUserStore()

const chatVisible = ref(false)
const chatTarget = ref(null)

const openPrivateChat = (user) => {
  chatTarget.value = { id: user.id, name: user.name }
  chatVisible.value = true
}

const openGroupChat = () => {
  chatTarget.value = null
  chatVisible.value = true
}

const myRoom = ref(null)
const myBed = ref(null)
const myBuilding = ref(null)
const roommates = ref([])
const checkInDate = ref('-')
const dash = ref({})

const fetchDashboard = async () => {
  try {
    const res = await request({ url: '/dashboard/dorm', method: 'get' })
    if (res) {
      dash.value = res
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.error(e)
  }
}

// Combine me + roommates into one list for rendering
const allOccupants = computed(() => {
  const list = []
  const me = userStore.userInfo
  if (me && myBed.value) {
    list.push({
      id: me.id,
      name: me.name,
      avatar: me.avatar,
      bedNumber: myBed.value.bedNumber,
      isMe: true
    })
  }
  list.push(...roommates.value)
  // Sort by bed number
  list.sort((a, b) => {
    const numA = parseInt(a.bedNumber.split('-')[1] || 0)
    const numB = parseInt(b.bedNumber.split('-')[1] || 0)
    return numA - numB
  })
  return list
})

const getOccupantForBed = (bedIndex) => {
  if (!myRoom.value) return null
  const targetBedSuffix = `-${bedIndex}`
  return allOccupants.value.find(u => u.bedNumber.endsWith(targetBedSuffix))
}

const fetchDormInfo = async () => {
  try {
    const [bedsRes, usersRes, roomsRes, buildingsRes, stayHistory] = await Promise.all([
      getBeds(), getUsers(), getRooms(), getBuildings(),
      request({ url: '/stayHistory/current', method: 'get' })
    ])
    if (stayHistory?.checkInDate) {
      checkInDate.value = new Date(stayHistory.checkInDate).toLocaleDateString('zh-CN')
    }
    const beds = Array.isArray(bedsRes) ? bedsRes : (bedsRes.data || [])
    const users = Array.isArray(usersRes) ? usersRes : (usersRes.data || [])
    const rooms = Array.isArray(roomsRes) ? roomsRes : (roomsRes.data || [])
    const buildings = Array.isArray(buildingsRes) ? buildingsRes : (buildingsRes.data || [])

    const me = userStore.userInfo || {}
    const myBedInfo = beds.find(b => b.studentId === me.id)
    if (!myBedInfo) return // No bed assigned
    
    myBed.value = myBedInfo
    const roomInfo = rooms.find(r => r.id === myBedInfo.roomId)
    if (roomInfo) {
      myRoom.value = roomInfo
      myBuilding.value = buildings.find(b => b.id === roomInfo.buildingId)
      
      // Find roommates
      const roomBeds = beds.filter(b => b.roomId === roomInfo.id && b.studentId && b.studentId !== me.id)
      const userMap = Object.fromEntries(users.map(u => [u.id, u]))
      
      roommates.value = roomBeds.map(b => {
        const u = userMap[b.studentId] || {}
        return {
          id: u.id,
          name: u.name || '鏈煡',
          avatar: u.avatar,
          sno: u.username || '',
          bedNumber: b.bedNumber,
          isMe: false
        }
      })
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to fetch dorm info', e)
  }
}

onMounted(() => {
  fetchDormInfo()
  fetchDashboard()
})
</script>

          </template>
          <el-form :model="form" label-width="80px">
            <el-form-item label="鍙嶉绫诲瀷" required>
              <el-select v-model="form.type" placeholder="璇烽€夋嫨" style="width: 100%">
                <el-option label="鎰忚寤鸿" value="鎰忚寤鸿" />
                <el-option label="鎶曡瘔涓炬姤" value="鎶曡瘔涓炬姤" />
                <el-option label="鐢熸椿姹傚姪" value="鐢熸椿姹傚姪" />
                <el-option label="鍏朵粬" value="鍏朵粬" />
              </el-select>
            </el-form-item>
            <el-form-item label="鍙嶉鍐呭" required>
              <el-input v-model="form.content" type="textarea" :rows="5" placeholder="璇疯缁嗘弿杩版偍鐨勫弽棣堝唴瀹? />
            </el-form-item>
            <el-form-item>
              <el-button type="primary" @click="handleSubmit" :loading="submitting">鎻愪氦鍙嶉</el-button>
            </el-form-item>
          </el-form>
        </el-card>
      </el-col>

      <el-col :span="14">

            <div class="card-header">

            </div>

          <div v-loading="loading">
            <el-empty v-if="myFeedbacks.length === 0" description="鏆傛棤鍘嗗彶鍙嶉" />

                  {{ f.status === 'REPLIED' ? '宸插洖澶? : '鏈' }}
                </el-tag>

<script setup>
import { ref, onMounted } from 'vue'
import { MessageCircle } from '@lucide/vue'
import { useUserStore } from '../../store/user'
import { ElMessage } from 'element-plus'
import request from '../../utils/request'

const userStore = useUserStore()
const form = ref({ type: '鎰忚寤鸿', content: '' })
const myFeedbacks = ref([])
const loading = ref(false)
const submitting = ref(false)

const fetchMyFeedbacks = async () => {
  loading.value = true
  try {
    const res = await request({ 
      url: '/businessRecord/list', 
      method: 'get', 
      params: { creatorId: userStore.userInfo?.id } 
    })
    myFeedbacks.value = (res || []).filter(item => item.type === 'feedback')
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error('鑾峰彇鍙嶉璁板綍澶辫触')
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}

const handleSubmit = async () => {
  if (!form.value.content) {
    ElMessage.warning('璇峰～鍐欏弽棣堝唴瀹?)
    return
  }
  submitting.value = true
  try {
    await request({
      url: '/businessRecord/save',
      method: 'post',
      data: {
        creatorId: userStore.userInfo?.id,
        type: 'feedback',
        title: form.value.type,
        description: form.value.content,
        status: 'PENDING'
      }
    })
    ElMessage.success('鍙嶉鎻愪氦鎴愬姛')
    form.value.content = ''
    fetchMyFeedbacks()
  } catch (e) {
    ElMessage.error('鍙嶉鎻愪氦澶辫触')
  } finally {
    submitting.value = false
  }
}

onMounted(() => {
  if (userStore.userInfo?.id) fetchMyFeedbacks()
})
</script>

<style scoped>
.feedback-container { max-width: 1200px; margin: 0 auto; }
.hero-card { margin-bottom: 24px; border-radius: 12px; background: linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(var(--el-color-primary-rgb), 0.1), rgba(var(--el-color-primary-rgb), 0.02)); border: none; }
